# 获取当前账号有效的线下物流列表

```yaml

  title: ''
  description: ''
  version: 1.0.0
paths:
  /open/v1/order/logistics_agent/manage/get_enable_offline_product_list:
    post:
      summary: 获取当前账号有效的线下物流列表
      deprecated: false
      description: 三方物流商以及三方仓
      tags:
        - 开放平台/包裹/履约/基础管理
      parameters:
        - name: timerToken
          in: query
          description: ''
          example: '{{timerToken}}'
          schema:
            type: string
        - name: Cookie
          in: header
          description: ''
          example: '{{header_cookie}}'
          schema:
            type: string
      requestBody:
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              properties:
                logisticsAgentId:
                  type: integer
                  title: 物流id
                opOrderPackageIds:
                  type: array
                  items:
                    type: integer
                    description: 包裹id
                  title: 包裹ids
              x-apifox-orders:
                - logisticsAgentId
                - opOrderPackageIds
              x-apifox-ignore-properties: []
            example: "{\r\n    \"logisticsAgentId\": 573,\r\n    \"opOrderPackageIds\": [\r\n        043701\r\n    ]\r\n}"

        '200':
          description: ''
          content:
```
